Transmuting certainty to brittle glass,  
She draws me down a bright and winding stream,  
Where every shade and echo seems to pass.  
Yet still I chase her ever-changing face,  
Entranced by visions spun from longing’s thread,  
Though reason’s hand would lead me from this place,  
Her glamour holds me fast where I am led.  
  O, shall I pierce the spell that she has spun,  
  Or love the lie, and never see the sun?

999

Illusion weaves her gauze about my soul,  
A silent wraith that keeps thee from my side,  
She drapes the dawn in shadows, takes her toll,  
And paints thee distant on the morning tide.  
Her whispered lies like silver threads entwine  
Around the truth my waking heart would see,  
She dims the sun and bids the stars resign,  
And steals thy voice from every memory.  
Yet through this mist of longing and deceit,  
Thy image burns as constant as the flame,  
No phantom power can render thee defeat,  
Nor cloak the music of thy cherished name.  
  Though specters rise, my soul shall still believe—  
  Beyond illusion, love will not deceive.

1000

Illusion weaves her veil about my soul,  
Her phantom hands do bid my heart obey,  
She paints with shadow, deftly takes control,  
And turns the noonday sun to ashen gray.  
A silver whisper threads through all my thought,  
Dissolving reason’s edge with dulcet lies,  
My truest self, bewildered and distraught,  
Finds comfort in her softly spoken sighs.  
Yet, every promise glimmers, then decays,  
Like morning dew that vanishes by noon.  
I chase the specter through bewildering maze,  
Entranced by hope that fades too soon.  
  Still, gentle dream, I know thou art untrue—  
  But gladly wear thy shade, and call it blue.

1001

Mine eyes, deceiv’d by love’s elusive shade,  
Doth lead my soul where falsehoods softly tread,  
A mist of longing on my heart is laid,  
While hope and reason war within my head.  
The gentle smile that bids my spirits rise  
Is but a mask that hides uncertain truth,  
Yet still I chase the promise in thine eyes,  
And seek the tender dreams I knew in youth.  
Oft have I wandered, lost in passion’s maze,  
Entranced by whispers sweet as summer rain,  
And built my trust on ever-changing days,  
Unmindful of the lurking seed of pain.  
  Yet though I stumble, blinded by desire,  
  Thy phantom love consumes me like a fire.

1002

Illusion weaves her veils about my sight,  
And courts my soul with shadows, false yet fair,  
She leads me, trembling, through the gilded night,  
And paints sweet dreams upon the misty air.  
Her whispers, silver-tongued, enchant my mind,  
Promising wonders just beyond my grasp,  
While truth, in humble garments, stays behind,  
And slips away unseen from out my clasp.  
Each fleeting vision dances, swift and bright,  
A phantom hope that shivers into gloom;  
Yet still I chase the promise of her light,  
Though reason warns me of impending doom.  
  O, gentle lie, thy beauty bids me stay—  
  And gladly blind, I follow where you stray.

1003

Illusion weaves her silken hands through me,  
A phantom queen that rules my waking mind,  
She fashions realms that none but I can see,  
And binds my sight with visions, deftly twined.  
Her whispered promises, like summer rain,  
Fall soft upon the furrows of my thought,  
She draws me gently toward joy or pain,  
And tints my memory with dreams she’s wrought.  
Each shadowed hope she paints with golden hue,  
Yet slips away when daylight dares appear,  
Her fragile truths dissolve to false from true,  
And leave me grasping phantoms, thin and sheer.  
  Yet though she fades as morning lights the sky,  
  Her spell endures, and still enchants my eye.

1004

Illusion weaves her silken shroud o’er me,  
Her whispered lies become my soul’s decree.  
I chase the shadows dancing on the wall,  
Each phantom promise, sweet and bittersweet.  
A silvered mask conceals what I might be,  
While dreams dissolve like dew beneath my feet.  
The painted dawn denies the coming night,  
Yet hope’s frail lantern flickers through the haze.  
I reach for truth, but grasp at shifting light,  
Entranced by echoes lost in memory’s maze.  
Yet though her veil may blind my waking eyes,  
A deeper wisdom stirs beneath her art:  
  For every web she spins, a spirit sighs,  
  And longing weaves its longing through my heart.

1005

O gentle shade, sweet Illusion, thou dost weep,  
Upon my heart, where silent sorrows dwell,  
Thy gossamer sighs disturb the halls of sleep,  
And in the hush, they toll a secret knell.  
Yet in thy arms, false comfort oft is found,  
A silken balm for wounds that bleed unseen;  
Though hope, like mist, dissolves without a sound,  
Still do I cling to dreams of might-have-been.  
Thy phantom hands entwine my waking soul,  
And lead me through the gardens of regret;  
Each blossom there, a memory and toll,  
Each petal moist with longing’s heavy debt.  
  Yet, Illusion, though thy tears unceasing flow,  
  I kiss their salt, and let my sadness grow.

1006

Illusion weaves her veil about mine eyes,  
And woos my heart with shadows in her train,  
She paints with silver mist the earth and skies,  
And whispers hope where reason would restrain.  
Her gentle hands transform the dusk to gold,  
And draw sweet shapes from corners of the mind;  
She bids the timid soul be brave and bold,  
And teaches joy to those whom fate unkind.  
Yet oft her charm dissolves with morning’s gleam,  
And leaves me grasping at the empty air,  
Remembrance clings to traces of the dream,  
While waking hours must bear their sober care.  
  Still, though her phantoms vanish with the dew,  
  I chase illusion, for her lies seem true.

1007

Illusion, thou dost weave thy silken snare,  
About my soul, in shadows of despair,  
Thy phantom fingers twine within my mind,  
And paint false dawns where hope is left behind.  
Thou art a mist that veils what eyes should see,  
A glass that warps the shape of verity;  
With honeyed tongue, thy promises entice,  
Yet leave the heart to pay a bitter price.  
In moonlit hours, thy whispers softly call,  
And bid me chase pale dreams that fade and fall;  
Yet still I follow, captive to thy art,  
Entranced by visions conjured in my heart.  
  O, gentle fraud, thy magic will I rue,  
  When morning breaks and shows thy lies anew.

1008

Thou art the dream that haunts my waking sight,  
A phantom sun that steals the shade of night,  
Thy whisper lingers soft as morning air,  
Thy absence sharp as frost upon the pane.  
Though distance draws its veil and keeps thee far,  
Within my breast thy memory doth remain.  
Each hope, a candle lit against the dark,  
Each fear, a shadow cast beneath thy star.  
If time be cruel and fates deny their grace,  
Still shall I seek thee in each dawning face,  
For love, though bound by neither time nor place,  
Endures beyond the reach of earth’s embrace.  
  So let the world turn blind and cold and vast—  
  My heart, thy dream, shall hold thee to the last.

1009

Thou art the shade where daylight seems to gleam,  
Yet night enfolds thy form in veiling light,  
A silken hush that dances through my dream,  
And turns the edge of sorrow into bright.  
Thy presence weaves a quiet, gentle tune  
Across the silent hours of the eve,  
Where silvered rays are borrowed from the moon  
And whispered hopes are summoned to believe.  
Though shadows stretch and time slips ever on,  
Thy memory like lanterns softly glows;  
Within my heart, the dusk and dawn are one,  
For in thy shade, a constant comfort grows.  
  So let the world in restless motion fade—  
  I find my peace within thy tender shade.

1010

Thou art the dream that mocks my waking sight,  
A phantom shade that shines with borrowed day,  
Thy whisper lingers deep within the night,  
Yet from my grasp thou ever slip’st away.  
I reach for thee in silent, trembling air,  
And find but echoes of thy gentle song,  
Thy absence weaves a sorrow I must bear,  
A silent ache that lingers all night long.  
Thy visage haunts the corners of my mind,  
A fleeting shadow cast by moon’s soft gleam,  
In waking hours thy memory I find,  
As real as sunlit dew, as false as dream.  
  Yet still, my soul pursues thee, ever true—  
  For all my worlds begin and end with you.

1011

Thou art the dream that haunts my waking eyes,  
Yet vanish’st swift whene’er I seek thee near;  
Thy shadow lingers soft ‘neath morning skies,  
But fades and leaves me lost in silent fear.  
Thy voice, remembered, rings in empty halls,  
A phantom echo woven through the air;  
I chase thy laughter down these memory walls,  
Yet find but dust and longing waiting there.  
Like moonlight slipping from a trembling hand,  
Or petals drifting on a midnight breeze,  
Thy presence slips away, I cannot stand  
Against the tide that sweeps thee from my pleas.  
  Still, in my heart thy gentle ghost remains—  
  A dream of love that time cannot restrain.

1012

Thou art the shadow dancing in mine eye,  
Yet substance flees whene’er I reach to hold.  
A fleeting wisp beneath the twilight sky,  
Thy whispered presence neither warm nor cold.  
In dreams thou linger’st, gentle as the mist,  
That curls upon the meadow’s waking breast,  
Yet waking hours know not that thou exist,  
Save as a longing never put to rest.  
Thy laughter echoes soft within my mind,  
A melody that haunts the silent night,  
And though I seek thee, ever am I blind,  
Chasing the ghost that slips beyond my sight.  
  Still, in this chase my heart doth find its art—  
  To love the shade that stays and will not part.

1013

Within thine eyes, a world of dreams doth lie,  
Yet truth, unkind, doth haunt where phantoms gleam.  
The silvered moon betrays the velvet sky,  
And dawn invades the castle of a dream.  
A whisper stirs the silence of the night,  
While hope in fragile slumber takes its flight.  
My heart, ensnared by shadows’ sweet deceit,  
Finds solace in illusions incomplete.  
Though gentle stars in silent counsel burn,  
Their distant gaze doth kindle faint surmise:  
That love, once lost, by longing may return  
And mend the wounds reflected in thine eyes.  
  Yet time, relentless river, bears me on—  
  Still, in thy gaze, the dream is never gone.

1014

Within the glass where shadows softly gleam,  
My soul debates the truth beneath the lie,  
Reflections fractured in a silver stream,  
A thousand faces pass and question why.  
The candle’s flame, uncertain in its glow,  
Distorts the visage gazing through the haze;  
I search for self in depths I cannot know,  
Each thought a ripple lost within the maze.  
Yet hope persists amid the dark’s embrace,  
A whisper carried by the silent night,  
That gently soothes the furrows from my face  
And coaxes reason closer to the light.  
  What I behold is both myself and more:  
  A soul unmasked upon the glassy shore.

1015

Within the glass of dreams mine own soul peers,  
Yet sees itself in shifting veils of night;  
The shadows whisper softly to mine ears,  
Unfolding secret hopes in silver light.  
A thousand faces flicker, fade, return,  
Each one a phantom memory of me,  
In silent longing do my senses burn,  
Desiring truth, yet fearing what may be.  
Dost thou not know the ache of such disguise,  
When heart and visage war beneath the skin?  
Reflections lie, yet truth within them lies,  
A paradox where dreams and doubts begin.  
  Yet as the dawn distills the dark away,  
  I find myself anew with every day.

1016

Within the glass of self, mine image fades,  
Yet truth and shadow in my soul are twined;  
A thousand faces dance in shifting shades,  
Wherein my heart its secret self doth find.  
The mirror’s surface ripples with the years,  
Each wrinkle whispers tales I dare not tell;  
Reflections blurred by laughter, washed by tears,  
Reveal the tides on which my spirits swell.  
Oft have I sought to pierce the misted pane  
And seize the core that flickers out of view,  
But fleeting forms and memories remain—  
A tapestry of hopes I thought I knew.  
  Yet in this glass, my likeness is made whole,  
  A mingling light and dark that shapes my soul.

1017

Within the glassy realm where shadows play,  
Mine aching soul doth chase a phantom light,  
The silver specters flit and fade away,  
Yet hope compels me onward through the night.  
Upon the pane, pale moonbeams softly gleam,  
Their trembling fingers beckon from afar;  
I wander lost within this midnight dream,  
Led on by whispers of a distant star.  
The silent hours stretch with longing’s weight,  
As time dissolves in pools of memory;  
I grasp at echoes, but they will not wait,  
Each promise drifts beyond my reach to be.  
  Yet still I seek, though darkness doth enthrall,  
  For in that search, my soul remembers all.

1018

Mine eye doth see what heart would fain disprove,  
A phantom’s grace in shadows we pursue,  
The silent whispers of a vanished love  
Do haunt the halls where once sweet promise grew.  
What gentle art can paint the ghostly form  
That flits between the longing and the real?  
A breath, a touch, a memory grown warm—  
All lost to time’s relentless, turning wheel.  
Yet hope, that stubborn weed, doth rise anew,  
Defiant in the garden of my pain;  
It drinks the dew, and dreams the sky is blue,  
Unheeding of the ever-falling rain.  
  O heart, accept the truth thy eye hath shown:  
  To chase mere shade is to be left alone.

1019

Mine eyes perceive a phantom clothed in light,  
Yet know my soul the shadowed truth of night.  
The gilded veil that shimmers o’er the scene  
Hides tempests raging where the world has been.  
A gentle smile, so sweetly worn and fair,  
Conceals the ache that lingers deep inside.  
Though radiance may gild the midnight air,  
No borrowed glow can wounded hearts provide.  
I walk the corridors of dreams and doubt,  
Each step uncertain, haunted by a shade.  
The golden mask, so bold and yet devout,  
Cannot make day from darkness softly laid.  
  Yet hope endures, though night and light contend—  
  That dawn may heal the truths we dare not mend.

1020

Within the mirror’s glass I seek my shade,  
Yet false reflections haunt my waking mind,  
A shifting form in borrowed light arrayed,  
Whose secret truths I struggle yet to find.  
The silvered pane with silent voice denies  
The visage I remember as my own;  
It mocks my hope with ever-changing eyes,  
And whispers fears I dare not face alone.  
Each morning’s glance unveils a subtle change;  
A wrinkle deepens, or a color pales.  
Familiar features subtly rearrange,  
As time’s relentless tide o’er youth prevails.  
  Yet in this glass, though fleeting forms may fall,  
  The soul endures, unshadowed, through it all.

1021

Mine eyes perceive what truth they dare not see,  
For shadowed self in glass doth counterfeit.  
A flicker’s veil divides the real from me,  
And masks my soul in shapes that do not fit.  
The silvered pane returns a borrowed guise,  
Yet ever strange the gaze that meets my own.  
Within reflection, mystery belies  
The silent voice for which my heart has grown.  
Oft have I wondered if the form I bear  
Is but projection of unspoken dreams,  
Or if the light reveals what’s truly there  
When visage shimmers on the glass’s gleam.  
  Thus, in each mirrored glance I seek to find  
  The hidden depths that dwell within the mind.

1022

Within the glass I spy a shadowed face,  
Yet know not if the form be truth or guise,  
A fleeting presence in a silvered space,  
That haunts the corners of my searching eyes.  
Its gaze is mine and yet it seems estranged,  
A mimicry that dances in the light,  
As moonlit shapes upon the wall are changed  
By subtle winds that stir the soul at night.  
What secrets linger in that silent stare,  
What whispered hopes or sorrows lie concealed?  
Does time distort the image gazing there,  
Or is the self in shadow more revealed?  
  So stands the glass, a portal and a mask,  
  To question who I am, I dare not ask.

1023

Mine eyes do drink the subtle draught of lies,  
Yet crave thy truth beneath thy shadowed mien,  
A veil of dusk conceals thy bright disguise,  
Yet hope persists where honesty hath been.  
Thy words, like honeyed arrows, pierce my trust,  
And paint with golden hue what should be clear;  
Yet in thy gaze I find a gentle gust  
That blows away the fog of doubt and fear.  
Oft have I wandered, seeking proof in vain,  
To reconcile thy visage and thy voice,  
Yet in my heart, despite the silent pain,  
Affection lingers, not a conscious choice.  
  Thus do I cherish shadows thou hast cast,  
  For in their depth, my love for thee holds fast.

1024

Within the glass I spy a phantom face,  
Yet know the shade doth mock my yearning heart;  
Its hollow eyes reflect my own disgrace,  
A pale reminder we are worlds apart.  
The candle’s glow reveals what night conceals,  
Soft whispers trailing through the silent air,  
Each breath of hope another sorrow steals,  
As shadows tangle softly in my hair.  
What longing aches behind that mirrored sheen,  
What silent tales the silvered depths contain?  
Unwritten dreams on surfaces unseen,  
Desires adrift like memories in rain.  
  Yet still I gaze and seek in fragile light,  
  A promise formed within the arms of night.

1025

Mine eyes perceive sweet forms that are not thine,  
Yet heart deceived doth pine for love’s cruel jest,  
A shadowed hope in moonlit dreams doth shine,  
Whilst waking hours deny my soul its rest.  
The zephyrs whisper secrets through the trees,  
Of all the vows and promises unkept,  
Their rustling sighs borne softly on the breeze,  
Recall the bitter tears that I have wept.  
Fair visions haunt the edges of my thought,  
Their fleeting smiles more piercing than a dart,  
Though reason bids me turn from loves long sought,  
Desire clings, relentless, to my heart.  
  O, would that I could break affection’s chain,  
  And let my spirit rise, unbound by pain.

1026

Your eyes, a glass through which my heart deceives,  
Reflecting truths I wish were only lies,  
In silent depths, a thousand secret eves  
Unfold beneath the shadow of your sighs.  
They glimmer with the dawn I dare not seek,  
Yet drown my dreams in twilight's gentle shade,  
A language soft, unspoken, and unique,  
Where hope and longing waltz and then do fade.  
Each glance a tempest, tender and unkind,  
Where joy and sorrow weave a tangled thread,  
I search for answers I may never find  
Within the stories that your gaze has said.  
  Yet in your eyes, despite my heart’s unrest,  
  I see the truth that loves me nonetheless.

1027

Your image shimmers, woven out of haze,  
A silver ghost that flickers in my mind,  
It haunts the corners of my waking days,  
A gentle echo time cannot unbind.  
The moonlight paints your smile on empty walls,  
While memories like trailing shadows creep;  
Each whispered thought your vanished voice recalls,  
And stirs the heart from fitful, restless sleep.  
The dusk unfolds your laughter in the air,  
A melody too fragile to contain,  
I reach for you—find only empty care,  
Yet in your absence, longing must remain.  
  Still, hope endures, though daylight steals you far:  
  I chase your light, my ever-fading star.

1028

I chase the shadow where your laughter fell,  
A painted ghost that haunts my waking mind,  
Each echo weaves a half-remembered spell  
Of golden hours the present cannot find.  
The sunlit air retains a vanished trace  
Of joy that fluttered once within your eyes;  
Now silence fills the hollow of this place  
Where memory and longing crystallize.  
I search the hush for whispers of your name,  
But time has blurred the footprints that you left;  
The garden blooms, yet nothing is the same—  
Each blossom tinged with beauty and with theft.  
  Yet hope persists, though absence is my foe:  
  Your laughter’s shade still teaches me to grow.

1029

Your laughter shimmers, glass upon the sand,  
A fragile mask I cradle in my gaze.  
Each gleaming note escapes my careful hand,  
And scatters light in ever-shifting haze.  
Beneath the surface, shadows softly play,  
Where silent fears like drifting seaweed lie;  
Yet in your smile, the evening turns to day,  
And troubled truths grow gentle by and by.  
The tide of time may sweep our prints away,  
But echoes linger in the whispered foam—  
A memory the restless waves convey,  
That finds its form when hearts are far from home.  
  So let me hold this laughter, bright and brief,  
  A jeweled echo on the shore of grief.

1030

Your face, a phantom flickers in my mind,  
A painted truth I never seem to hold,  
It haunts the hollow corners left behind  
Where whispered longing turns the air to gold.  
The night recalls the curve of shadowed cheek  
With moonlit grace upon a distant shore,  
And though I search, the words I dare not speak  
Are scattered petals fading evermore.  
I chase the echo sung in silent halls,  
Each memory a spark against the gloom,  
Yet time, relentless, weaves its shrouding thralls  
And seals your likeness in a shadowed room.  
  Still, hope endures where dreams and daylight part—  
  Your phantom lingers, painted on my heart.

1031

Your eyes, twin lanterns veiled behind the mist,  
Project a world where hope and longing blend,  
They spark in shadow, barely to exist,  
Yet draw my soul where waking dreams ascend.  
Within their gaze, the restless stars ignite  
And scatter silver secrets through the night;  
I gaze and find my own reflection there,  
A silent wish adrift on trembling air.  
Each glance becomes a promise left unspoke,  
A gentle storm that stirs the sleeping sea;  
The hush between us, fragile as the smoke,  
Conceals a thousand fates that long to be.  
  Still, in your eyes, dawn’s certainty I see—  
  And in their light, my heart is ever free.

1032

My soul is but a mirror lost in mist,  
Reflecting ghosts that never once exist,  
Each fleeting shape by fog and longing kissed,  
A phantom memory the mind has missed.  
Uncertain forms arise and softly fade,  
Their whispers echo in the hollow air,  
Between the dusk and dawn, in silver shade,  
They haunt the silence with a gentle care.  
Yet as the morning breaks with golden fire,  
The veils dissolve, the shadows slip away,  
And hope, reborn, ascends a little higher,  
Inviting light to fill the hollowed gray.  
  My soul, though veiled, still seeks the sun’s embrace,  
  To find itself anew in clearer space.

1033

My soul, a fragile mirror veiled in mist,  
Reflects a world that never quite exists,  
Each breath a fleeting shadow, gently kissed  
By longing's lips, in silent, aching twists.  
The dawn arrives with promises unmet,  
Its golden fingers trembling on the pane,  
While memories, like dew, in silence fret,  
And sorrow weaves its silver through the rain.  
The hours drift by on wings of quiet doubt,  
Their echoes lost in corridors of thought,  
Yet hope, a stubborn seed, begins to sprout  
Within the cracks that solitude has wrought.  
  Though veiled and vague, my heart strives to believe  
  That light endures in all that we conceive.

1034

My soul becomes a shadow on the wall,  
A flicker cast by light that cannot stay,  
It dances with the dusk, so thin and small,  
And slowly fades as night devours the day.  
What substance have I, save this ghostly trace  
That wavers with the candle’s dying gleam?  
I reach for warmth, but emptiness I chase,  
My hopes dissolve into a silent dream.  
Yet in this darkness, longing finds its voice,  
A whisper clings to every trembling line,  
Proclaiming in the void a secret choice:  
To shape my shade with meaning, though I pine.  
  If light returns, my shadow will remain—  
  A memory of loss, and hope through pain.

1035

My soul, a mirror clouded by your gaze,  
Reflects a dream that flickers, then decays.  
Thine eyes, twin lanterns shining through the mist,  
Recall the dawn my lonely heart had missed.  
In silent halls where memory holds sway,  
Thy whisper lingers, haunting every stone.  
The golden hours, once bright, now fade to gray,  
And leave me wandering midnight and alone.  
O, gentle ghost, why dost thou shadow me,  
With phantoms woven from remembered light?  
Each hour I chase what nevermore shall be,  
And mourn the sun eclipsed by endless night.  
  Yet in this gloom, thy image still appears,  
  A star that glimmers in the veil of tears.

1036

My soul, a lantern veiled in misted glass,  
Yearns for your light it never can possess.  
Through shadowed halls where silent moments pass,  
I wander, burdened by this loneliness.  
A distant star, you shimmer in my night,  
Unreachable, yet guiding with your fire,  
Each step I take is drawn unto your light,  
Each breath inflamed by secret, fierce desire.  
If fate were kind, our paths might intertwine,  
And dawn would break upon my longing face;  
But fortune’s threads refuse your hand in mine,  
And leave me drifting in this empty space.  
  Yet hope endures, though night and sorrow blend:  
  Your radiance within me shall not end.

1037

My soul, a lantern lost in fog’s embrace,  
Pursues a phantom love it cannot hold,  
It seeks thy shadowed form from place to place,  
While memories grow faint and feelings cold.  
Each whispered hope dissolves in silent mist,  
Yet still I wander, yearning for thy light,  
Recalling every moment that we kissed,  
Though daybreak turns my longing into night.  
The mournful moon upon the river gleams,  
A witness to my ever-haunted quest,  
Reflected in the glass of sleepless dreams  
Where visions of thy beauty grant no rest.  
  Yet should thy heart return, these clouds would part—  
  And dawn would kindle fire within my heart.

1038

I wear a mask of glass to shield my soul,  
Reflections crack beneath the weight I bear,  
Each glance reveals a fragment, not the whole,  
A haunted gleam of longing and despair.  
The world demands a visage pure and bright,  
A flawless surface free from stain or scar,  
Yet fissures grow in silence, out of sight,  
Like distant thunder rumbling from afar.  
I fear the shatter, dread the sharp reveal  
Of all the hidden pain I strive to hide,  
But glass, though fragile, teaches hearts to feel  
The beauty in the flaws we hold inside.  
  So let the mask be splintered, let it fall—  
  In shards of truth, at last, I stand, grown tall.

1039

Each morning paints a mask upon my skin,  
While shadows whisper truths I keep within.  
The mirror’s gaze reflects what I pretend,  
Yet silent doubts like vines begin to spin.  
Upon my brow, a borrowed light I wear,  
To greet the world with borrowed, borrowed grace,  
But in the hush of dawn’s unguarded air,  
I meet the haunted contours of my face.  
Beneath the gloss of smiles I have rehearsed,  
A thousand secret selves remain unseen.  
The brightest day reveals what night reversed,  
A fragile peace that falters in between.  
  Yet hope endures, though veiled in shifting hue—  
  One day, my mask may crack, and I shine through.

1040

The mirror’s glass distorts the face I wear,  
A phantom self reflected in my gaze;  
It warps the lines that time and worry bear,  
And shrouds my eyes in memory’s shifting haze.  
What visage do I own, and which is lie?  
The silvered pane reveals what I must doubt:  
A borrowed mask that shivers when I sigh,  
A silent echo peering from without.  
Yet still I search those depths for something true,  
A glimmer of the soul beneath the skin,  
A whispered hope that morning might renew  
The faded gleam that once had burned within.  
  Though glass may trick, my heart remains sincere—  
  The truth I seek is ever living here.

1041

A shimmered glass reflects a borrowed face,  
I chase the shade of love that will not stay,  
The morning sun recedes with gentle pace,  
Its golden promises dissolve to gray.  
Within this frame, a thousand dreams reside,  
Each longing gaze entangles hope and fear,  
The whispered truths that silent lips confide  
Are scattered leaves that autumn draws too near.  
Yet in the mirror’s depth I search for more,  
A spark of self untouched by fleeting years,  
A secret light behind the bolted door,  
Unclouded by regret or salted tears.  
  Though time may steal the visage youth supplied,  
  The soul’s true image will not be denied.

1042

I wear a mask of laughter on my face,  
While shadows whisper truths I can’t embrace.  
The jester’s smile, a fragile, painted grace,  
Conceals the ache that time cannot erase.  
Beneath the gilded lines my sorrows dwell,  
Each jest a veil for grief I dare not show;  
Night’s silent hour my solemn secrets tell,  
Yet dawn demands I meet the world’s bright glow.  
I dance in daylight’s court, my heart disguised,  
A marionette upon the stage of men,  
And though my inner tears remain despised,  
I trace a grin and hide my pain again.  
  But oh, if ever mask and soul align,  
  Perhaps the laughter might at last be mine.

1043

My heart’s a glass, reflecting borrowed light,  
Yet shatters when you turn your gaze away.  
Within your eyes, the stars are burning bright,  
But absent warmth, the heavens fall to gray.  
Each word you speak, a gentle autumn breeze,  
That stirs the golden leaves inside my chest;  
Yet silence chills, and longing will not ease,  
For love unmet can never be confessed.  
I gather shards and trace your vanished face  
In trembling hands that dream of days once whole,  
While hope remains, a ghost I still embrace,  
And memory, the keeper of my soul.  
  If ever dawn should bid our shadows part,  
  Know you have left your sunlight in my heart.

1044

Illusion wears your face within my mind,  
She weaves your voice in shadows where I stray,  
A phantom touch in dreams I wake to find,  
And echoes of your laughter haunt the day.  
The golden light recalls the warmth you gave,  
Yet slips through grasping fingers, swift and sly.  
In memory’s glass, your gentle features wave,  
A vision neither truth nor honest lie.  
I chase your image down the winding lane  
Where hope and longing ever intertwine,  
Each footstep falling softly, drawn by pain,  
A silhouette against a dusk-lit sign.  
  Yet still, I seek the shadow of your grace,  
  For love endures, though time would it erase.

1045

Illusion drapes thy face in ghostly lace,  
My heart debates the truth it longs to see,  
A shadowed glance conceals thy gentle grace,  
Yet hope ignites a fragile flame in me.  
The moonlight weaves a silver web of doubt,  
Each whispered word a mask of shifting hue,  
Desire, cloaked in silence, wanders out  
To seek the soul it vainly thought it knew.  
If eyes could pierce the veils that secrets wear,  
And lips could speak the secrets fears confess,  
Would dawn reveal a vision bright and fair,  
Or strip away the comfort of this guess?  
  Yet still I chase thy specter through the night,  
  For love persists where truth eludes the light.

1046

Illusion drapes thee softly in its shade,  
A whisper clothed in longing’s silver thread,  
The fleeting shapes of dreams begin to fade,  
Yet haunt the silent chambers of thy head.  
Unseen, desire’s hand doth stroke thy hair,  
And paints with moonlit breath a phantom face,  
While shadows weave their secrets on the air,  
And time itself dissolves without a trace.  
If hope be but a shadow’s gentle art,  
Then let me hold its semblance in my arms,  
And cherish every echo in my heart  
Though morning strips the midnight of its charms.  
  For in illusion’s arms we sweetly lie,  
  And wake to find the truth in every sigh.

1047

Illusion weaves her hands through midnight air,  
She paints your shadow where no light is cast,  
Her whispered lies are filaments of care,  
A moonlit promise never meant to last.  
Her fingers shape the dark into a dream,  
And draw your longing forth with gentle art;  
She conjures hope from every silver seam,  
Yet leaves but empty chambers in your heart.  
Her laughter dances softly on the breeze,  
A haunting echo fading into mist;  
She tempts the soul with easeful, honeyed ease,  
Then vanishes, unseen and unkissed.  
  Beware the spell that darkness may bestow—  
  For what she gives, the dawn will never show.

1048

Illusion weaves her silk about your face,  
Her whispered promises dissolve in air,  
She leads thee gently to a painted place  
Where truth is masked and falsehood blossoms fair.  
Each hope she fashions shimmers in the gloom,  
A silvered thread that tangles heart and mind,  
Yet vanishes like roses out of bloom  
When reason’s dawn breaks gently from behind.  
Her laughter dances lightly on the breeze,  
A phantom echo lost beyond recall.  
She bids thee dream, yet dreams are but disease—  
Sweet venom in the veins that doth enthrall.  
  Yet from her snare we rise, though oft we fall,  
  And seek the light that dwells beyond her thrall.

1049

Illusion weaves your shadow through my mind,  
A silent ghost that will not let me go,  
Thy memory, in cruel threads, I find  
Entwined with every thought I come to know.  
A whisper lingers where thy laughter lay,  
And haunts the hush of every twilight hour;  
No sunbeam breaks the sorrow from my day,  
Nor dew revives my heart’s forgotten flower.  
Yet still, I chase thy phantom in the gloom,  
And beg the night to show me what was lost,  
But dreams dissolve as morning breaks their tomb,  
And daylight pays remembrance at a cost.  
  Though shadows fade, thy presence will remain—  
  A gentle ache, a sweet, enduring pain.

1050

Illusion drapes my soul in veils of mist,  
She whispers truths that shadows can't resist,  
Her silken words by moonlight softly kissed,  
Transform the world to dreamers' amethyst.  
Unfurling visions dance across the night,  
Concealing faults with gentle, spectral hands,  
She turns the harshest pain in golden light,  
And paints contentment over barren lands.  
Yet in her arms, reality grows thin—  
A fragile glass that cracks beneath a sigh.  
What once seemed certain now dissolves within  
A tapestry of secrets woven sly.  
  Though sweet her lies, I seek the dawn anew—  
  For only in the sun can I be true.

1051

Illusion weaves her fingers through my soul,  
She paints with shadows where my hopes reside,  
A whispered veil that makes the broken whole,  
Yet leads my weary heart where truths divide.  
She fashions golden dreams from brittle clay,  
And strings the stars in patterns never real,  
Her laughter dances just beyond the day,  
A silver promise none may touch or steal.  
I chase her footprints down the haunted lane,  
Each step dissolves beneath the morning’s eye,  
She leaves me longing, tender with the pain,  
While gentle lies drift softly from the sky.  
  Yet in her arms, I find a sweet reprieve—  
  For what is life, if not the dreams we weave?

1052

Illusion weaves her fingers through my soul,  
And paints with borrowed light what I perceive;  
She shapes the world, a shifting, spectral whole,  
And tricks my heart to trust what senses grieve.  
The dawn she gilds with hope’s deceptive gold,  
Yet dusk reveals the hollows in her art;  
Her silken whispers, sweet and manifold,  
Entangle reason, clouding mind and heart.  
Yet in her grasp I find a strange delight,  
For dreams are spun from threads she deftly winds;  
Each phantom joy that dances in my sight  
Unmasks the hidden longing of our minds.  
  Though truth may banish her with morning's gleam,  
  We live and love, enchanted by her dream.

1053

My soul, a jester draped in borrowed light,  
Performs for shadows cast by love’s retreat,  
With painted smile I dance away the night,  
While echoing footsteps mock my defeat.  
A crown of thorns concealed beneath my jest,  
I spin illusions on this haunted stage,  
Yet longing’s fire smolders in my chest,  
A captive bird that beats against its cage.  
The fickle moon observes with cold disdain,  
My revels wrought to mask a silent ache,  
Each jest a plea, each bow a secret pain,  
A laughter frail, too fragile not to break.  
  But when the dawn dissolves my motley guise,  
  Truth’s tearful dawn is mirrored in my eyes.

1054

Illusion weaves her fingers through my soul,  
She paints your absence with a lover’s hue,  
Each whispered hope, a ghost I can’t control,  
Yet in the dusk, your memory feels true.  
The silent shadows stretch along the floor,  
Entwined with dreams that tremble at the dawn,  
Your laughter echoes softly, evermore,  
A haunting echo long since faded, gone.  
I chase the specter of your gentle face,  
A vision born of longing’s restless art,  
Desire lingers in this empty space,  
A phantom’s kiss imprinted on my heart.  
  Though reason pleads, I will not let you go—  
  For even absence teaches love to grow.

1055

Illusion weaves her fingers through my soul,  
And paints a hope where love can never stay,  
She spins a gilded dream I can’t control,  
Then softly lures my trust to drift away.  
Her whispers echo sweet in shadowed halls,  
A tapestry of longing, deftly sewn,  
Yet every thread she crafts, deception calls,  
And leaves me wandering the dark alone.  
Her laughter glimmers false in morning light,  
Unraveling the vows I thought were true,  
A phantom’s touch that melts with coming night,  
Still draws me back to chase her fading hue.  
  Though reason warns against her siren art,  
  I cling to illusions that ensnare my heart.

1056

Illusion clasps my hand with silken lies,  
And paints my shadow brighter than my soul,  
It whispers dreams as truth behind my eyes,  
A subtle thief that claims a gentle toll.  
It weaves its golden thread through waking thought,  
And veils reality with honeyed hue,  
So tangled in the web that it has wrought,  
I drink the false for fact, the false for true.  
Yet in the hush between the dusk and dawn,  
When silence peels the mask from secret night,  
Its painted splendor pales, its spell is gone,  
And in the gloom I see by truer light.  
  Though illusion’s hand may guide me for a while,  
  I seek the dawn where shadows reconcile.

1057

Illusion weaves her fingers through my mind,  
A silent whisper shaping what I see,  
She draws a veil, my sight to half-confined,  
And paints with shadows what the truth might be.  
Her masquerade in moonlit fancy sways,  
A dancer cloaked in silver, fleet and sly,  
Transforming night to bright and bright to haze,  
The honest world recast within her eye.  
I chase the echoes of her phantom hand,  
Believing substance dwells in empty air,  
Yet waking finds my castles built on sand,  
And dawn dissolves the dreams that dwelt there.  
  Still, though I know her lies, I long to find  
  The gentle touch illusion leaves behind.

1058

Illusion weaves her fingers through my mind,  
She paints my soul with truths I cannot hold,  
Her whispers, soft as silk, my thoughts unwind,  
And turn my reason’s iron into gold.  
She dances in the corners of my sight,  
A shadowed waltz where certainty dissolves,  
She cloaks the dawn and veils the honest light,  
In mysteries no careful hand resolves.  
She draws me deeper into silver haze,  
Where dreams and memories entwine and blur,  
Each promise spun in labyrinthine maze,  
Where all is hers and nothing can be sure.  
  Yet in her arms, I rest and dare believe—  
  For sweetest lies are all we wish conceive.

1059

Illusion weaves her silver through my mind,  
A lover's ghost whose touch I cannot claim,  
She drifts in moonlight, beautiful and blind,  
A whispered thought that flickers into flame.  
Her laughter shivers softly on the breeze,  
Yet slips through grasping hands like morning mist,  
A phantom scent that teases, fails to please,  
A shadowed memory my heart insists  
Must still be real, though reason tells me no,  
For every dream is stitched with threads of lies.  
She dances just beyond the light’s faint glow,  
A star that falls but never truly dies.  
  I chase her through the labyrinth of sleep,  
  And wake with longing far too deep to keep.

1060

Illusion weaves her fingers through my mind,  
She paints your absent love in wistful hue,  
A phantom touch that memory designed,  
Yet leaves me thirsty for a love untrue.  
The moonlight spills upon this empty space,  
Where once your laughter danced upon the air;  
I search the shadows for your vanished face,  
And find but echoes, hollow with despair.  
Though reason bids my longing heart be still,  
Each thought returns to you, a haunted song;  
Desire rebels against my weary will,  
And drags me, captive, where old dreams belong.  
  Yet if illusion is the price I pay,  
  Let fantasy, not truth, attend my day.

1061

Illusion drapes my heart in borrowed light,  
She dances near, but ever out of reach,  
A wraith of hope that glimmers in my sight,  
Yet slips away whene'er I strive to teach  
My soul to trust the promise of her hand.  
Her laughter haunts the corridors of dreams,  
A melody I cannot understand,  
A river lost to ever-widening streams.  
What shadow paints her smile upon the wall,  
What gentle hush compels my restless mind?  
I chase her footsteps down the silent hall,  
But find but dust and echoes left behind.  
  Yet still I turn, compelled by sweet deceit,  
  And beg illusion's ghost my heart to meet.

1062

Thy laughter shines, a mask upon the gloom,  
Yet shadows linger where thy bright eyes play;  
A fleeting sun that dances through the room,  
But cannot chase the silent night away.  
Thy lips do curve as if to scorn the pain,  
While secret sorrows nestle in thy breast;  
Thy mirth a gentle, sweet and silver chain,  
That binds thy heart and will not let it rest.  
The world doth see the shimmer of thy smile,  
And takes the light for truth, the jest for peace;  
Yet in thine eyes, betray’d, for but a while,  
The silent storms that never find release.  
  O, would my love could lift thy mask from thee,  
  And grant thy soul the peace it longs to be.

1063

Your face, a lantern flickering through the mist,  
Yet truth behind the glow I oft resist.  
A beacon trembling where the shadows twist,  
Unknown, uncertain, shaped by longing’s tryst.  
Your eyes, twin stars that pierce the shroud of night,  
Invite my soul to wander, lost, unsure.  
Yet veiled in fog, they mask both fear and light,  
And leave my heart in ache no glance can cure.  
A whispered promise hidden in the gloom,  
A secret kept by lips that dare not speak,  
Within your smile both hope and dread find room,  
As silence falls and makes the future bleak.  
  Yet still I chase that lantern’s fleeting gleam,  
  And risk the dark for love’s uncertain dream.

1064

Thy smile, a phantom dancing in the mist,  
Yet real as longing none can e’er resist.  
It haunts the silent corridors of night,  
A lantern burning soft with secret light.  
Upon thy lips, the dawn’s first blush appears,  
Dispelling shadows, drying up my tears.  
No star can rival such a gentle gleam,  
Nor moonlight match the sweetness of thy beam.  
In every breath, I taste thy distant grace,  
And find the memory living in its place.  
Each glance, a whispered promise, half-confessed,  
That leaves my heart both troubled and at rest.  
  So let me chase this vision till I sleep,  
  For love’s illusion is the soul’s to keep.

1065

Your smile, a lantern in a world of shade,  
Yet all its light dissolves when I draw near;  
A trembling glow, by bashful moon betrayed,  
Retreats and leaves me prisoner to fear.  
Though stars conspire to gild your gentle face,  
Their silver threads unravel as I gaze,  
And hope, once bold, now falters in its place,  
Bewitched and blinded by your subtle blaze.  
If words could borrow fire from your delight,  
They’d kindle warmth to thaw this silent cold,  
But breath betrays me, vanquished by the night,  
And longing lingers, timid yet untold.  
     Yet still I stand, ensnared by beauty’s art,  
     A moth pursuing flame—my helpless heart.

1066

Your laughter weaves the sunlight through my shade,  
Yet shadows linger where your promise shone.  
A melody of warmth that once you played  
Now echoes softly, though the source is gone.  
Sweet memories, like petals, gently fall  
Upon the ground where once our springtime grew.  
I trace the silent pattern of it all,  
Each golden hour I cherished spent with you.  
Yet seasons turn, and with them, hope may fade,  
The garden's bloom replaced by autumn's sigh.  
Still, in the hush, your voice will not evade  
The corners of my heart that long to fly.  
  Though time may dim the colors we have known,  
  Your laughter’s sun will find me, not alone.

1067

You shine as truth, yet shadows veil your eyes,  
A love in form, but hollow in its core,  
The golden speech that softly testifies  
Conceals the silence I cannot ignore.  
Your laughter rings, yet echoes far and thin,  
A distant bell that trembles in the breeze,  
I search for warmth, but frost lies deep within,  
A gentle smile that harbors subtle tease.  
Though roses bloom upon your painted cheek,  
Their petals wilt beneath the weight of doubt;  
I seek your heart, but find it cold and meek,  
A wary soul that will not venture out.  
  Yet hope endures, though certainty is rare—  
  That time may teach your spirit how to care.

1068

My soul, a mirror cracked by wishful dreams,  
Reflects the truth in veils of silver lies,  
Each hope, a star that in the darkness gleams,  
Yet fades when dawn unveils its clear disguise.  
Desire’s hand does paint on glass so thin,  
Illusions dancing where the fractures meet;  
The haunted echoes whisper deep within,  
Where longing’s pulse and memory repeat.  
What shadows move behind the mirrored face,  
What silent tears upon its surface fall?  
The heart, in fragments, seeks its former grace,  
Yet dares not name the cost of longing’s thrall.  
  But still I gaze, though broken be the frame—  
  For in the shards, I find my truest name.

1069

My soul perceives what eyes can scarce believe,  
A world of shadows dancing in the light;  
The fleeting shapes that dawn and dusk conceive  
Slip through the fingers of the coming night.  
They whisper secrets in the silent air,  
While moonbeams gild the edges of a dream;  
Their voices hidden, yet their presence there,  
Like silver threads that bind the rushing stream.  
Oft have I chased their phantoms in my mind,  
And sought to grasp the substance of their song,  
But found their beauty veiled, their truth resigned  
To realms where mortal senses do not throng.  
  Yet still my soul, undaunted, seeks the gleam—  
  For shadows oft reveal the heart’s true theme.

1070

Within my soul, illusion weaves its snare,  
Yet truth persists and battles dreams that fade.  
A shadowed mist conceals what once was fair,  
While reason’s torch illuminates the shade.  
The phantoms dance upon the silent floor,  
Enticing hope with promises of gold,  
But wisdom knocks upon the hidden door,  
Demanding tales of honesty be told.  
Though whispering deceit may charm the ear,  
Its honeyed words dissolve with dawning light;  
For steadfast truth, however faint or sheer,  
Will banish falsehood’s specter from my sight.  
  Thus in this strife, my heart shall find its way—  
  By truth’s bright star, illusions fall away.

1071

My soul pursues the shadow of your grace,  
Yet finds but emptiness where hope once lay,  
The memory of light upon your face  
Still haunts the silent borders of my day.  
I wander paths where golden laughter died,  
And seek in wind-swept echoes your sweet name;  
The gentle dreams that once in me confide  
Now flicker, frail, and never burn the same.  
The night descends with whispers of regret,  
While longing weaves its shroud around my heart,  
No solace found in moon or sunbeam yet,  
Nor solace given by the world’s bright art.  
  Yet though I walk where shadows softly fall,  
  My soul recalls your grace, and loves through all.

1072

My soul wears veils where hope and sorrow blend,  
Illusions rise where love refused to stay,  
Each fragile dream bends close, as if to mend  
The wounds that memory cannot allay.  
Night’s gentle hand paints longing on the wall,  
While day reveals the cracks beneath the skin.  
The silent candle flickers in the hall,  
Its trembling light betrays the ache within.  
Yet from these shrouded depths a whisper grows,  
A promise seeded deep in barren ground;  
From silent tears, a wilder beauty flows,  
And in regret, a truer self is found.  
  Though shadows drape the corners of my mind,  
  Within the dark, new hope I strive to find.

1073

My soul, deceived by love's elusive art,  
Pursues a shadow, yearning for the sun.  
Though hope ignites a fire within my heart,  
Despair extinguishes what hope begun.  
Through tangled woods of longing do I stray,  
Entranced by whispers carried on the breeze,  
Each promise glimmering, then swept away,  
A fleeting vision lost among the trees.  
Yet still I wander, captive to the chase,  
Ensnared by dreams that shimmer, fade, and flee,  
The memory of laughter, and your face,  
The phantom touch that lingers haunting me.  
  But should the dawn reveal illusion’s cost,  
  Let wisdom bloom from all that I have lost.

1074

I chase the truth but find myself deceived,  
A shadow-self within the light believed.  
Each gleaming hope is cunningly reprieved,  
By doubts that in the silent dark are weaved.  
I seek a mirror honest as the sun,  
Yet see reflections fractured by my fears;  
The masks I wear and all the lies I’ve spun  
Distort my vision through the passing years.  
If wisdom’s torch could pierce the veiling mist,  
And show the soul that hides behind my gaze,  
Would I embrace the truths I have dismissed,  
Or turn from revelations that amaze?  
  Yet still I quest, though certainty is rare,  
  For truth’s elusive light I still would dare.

1075

I wear a mask of light to hide my shade,  
Yet truth and falsehood war within my mind,  
A gentle smile upon my lips is laid,  
Concealing shadows none may hope to find.  
Beneath the surface, tempests rage unseen,  
While outward calm compels the world’s belief;  
A painted grace where sorrow once has been,  
A borrowed joy disguising rooted grief.  
The dawn’s soft rays may gild my weary eyes,  
But twilight lingers deep behind my gaze;  
I walk in radiance spun from tender lies,  
A prisoner of my own deceitful maze.  
  Yet would I dare remove this bright façade,  
  And let the world behold my soul unclad?

1076

Within the mirror’s glass I seek my truth,  
Yet find a stranger’s mask upon my face,  
The lines that time has inked since restless youth  
Now twist and blur, impossible to trace.  
A thousand hopes reflected in my gaze  
Dissolve like mist before the morning sun;  
The self I was, now lost in memory’s haze,  
Contends with what my waking days have done.  
Yet still I search for meaning in the gleam  
That flickers faintly in these shadowed eyes,  
And wonder if the world is but a dream  
Wherein our fleeting visage softly lies.  
  O, grant me strength to know and to forgive—  
  To love the mirrored self I fear to live.

1077

I chase the shadow cast by lovers past,  
Yet hold the sun that never warms my skin,  
Each golden hour’s promise fading fast,  
While ghosts within my memory begin.  
Their whispered laughter lingers in the breeze,  
And petals fall where once our hearts did bloom.  
Desire’s echo drifts among the trees,  
A silent waltz that circles through my room.  
What folly, then, to mourn what’s gone before,  
And seek in present light what once was lost?  
Yet longing carves its patterns evermore,  
A tapestry entwined with love’s own cost.  
  Still, hope ignites anew with each day’s start,  
  And kindles gentle courage in my heart.

1078

I chase the shimmered ghost your smile became,  
Yet stumble through the truth my heart denies,  
Your laughter flickers, kindling hope’s brief flame,  
While shadows gather where my longing lies.  
The echo of your voice still haunts the air,  
In midnight’s hush, your memory returns,  
A perfume woven soft through dark despair,  
A candle’s gold that, unrelenting, burns.  
I number every hour since you’ve gone,  
Each star above a tear I cannot shed,  
I wear the silence like a mourning dawn,  
And find your absence heavy in my bed.  
  Yet still I dream you near, though fate forbids—  
  The heart remembers what the mind forbids.

1079

I chase the shadowed dream your heart denies,  
Yet hold a torch to what I know is smoke,  
The silent ache that flickers in my eyes  
Is kindled by the hope your words evoke.  
Though midnight weaves its doubts across my mind,  
And reason bids me let illusions fade,  
I raise my hands to stars I cannot find,  
Entranced by light that memory has made.  
I walk the garden paths where we once met,  
Each petal brushed by longing’s gentle breath,  
The dew-laced air still clinging with regret,  
A mourning song that trembles close to death.  
  Yet love persists, though night and reason part—  
  A phantom flame that haunts my foolish heart.

1080

Thou art the painted mist upon mine eyes,  
A phantom sweet, whose smile doth mask thy lies.  
Thy gentle words, like petals tossed in air,  
Conceal the thorns of secrets buried deep.  
I chase thy shadow, fleet beyond compare,  
While longing plants a vigil in my sleep.  
Thy laughter, silver-cast, rings through my mind,  
A haunting echo fading with the day.  
Yet in thy gaze, a world of woe I find,  
A midnight tide that sweeps my heart away.  
If love be but illusion, let it stay,  
For sweeter is the dream than waking pain.  
  Though truth may pale before the dawn’s new ray,  
  Within this painted mist I would remain.

1081

Thou art a phantom clothed in golden light,  
Yet every touch dissolves thee into shade;  
A flame that flickers just beyond my sight,  
A dream that dawn or reason quick betrayed.  
Thy laughter dances on the midnight air,  
And in its echo hope and longing grow;  
I reach, but find thee ever distant there,  
A star whose brilliance I may never know.  
Yet still I chase thy softly shining gleam,  
Enchanted by the promise in thy eyes,  
For in thy presence life becomes a dream,  
A tapestry of wonder and surprise.  
  Though thou art mist, thy memory remains—  
  A golden ghost that lingers in my veins.

1082

Thy visage fair, a glass of shifting mist,  
Doth mock the yearning heart that would insist  
Upon a form more constant to exist,  
Yet finds thy presence dancing in the twist  
Of morning’s gold or evening’s violet hue,  
Where shadows play and hope becomes unsure.  
My soul, a captive, seeks a path anew,  
But stumbles where thy beauty doth endure.  
O fickle light! Thou draw’st me to despair,  
And yet in darkness, still thy smile I seek;  
For though thou art as fleeting as the air,  
No mortal strength can make my longing weak.  
  So let me dwell within this gentle pain,  
  For love’s sweet ghost shall evermore remain.

1083

Thou art a phantom drawn from midnight’s glass,  
A shimmered hope that mocks the grasp of day,  
Thy silver breath upon the darkness pass,  
Dissolving dreams in pale and fleeting gray.  
When dawn’s bold fingers stroke the sleeping sky,  
Thou slip’st away on winds too soft to feel,  
Yet in my soul thy haunting shadows lie,  
A secret ache no morning can unseal.  
The world awakens, banishing thy shade,  
Yet in the hush between the sun and me,  
Thy whispered promises refuse to fade,  
A velvet ghost upon my memory.  
  Yet though thou vanish with the morning’s gleam,  
  I find thee ever dwelling in my dream.

1084

Thou art a phantom dancing in mine eye,  
A painted truth that mocks my heart’s desire,  
Thy whispered promise fades as breezes die,  
Yet in thy light my longing spirits tire.  
A shadowed hope doth haunt the silent night,  
Where dreams of thee like restless spectres roam,  
The moon doth pale before thy borrowed light,  
Yet still my thoughts return to thee, my home.  
What cruel jest that fate should weave thy face  
From distant stars and morning’s silver gleam,  
To grant me glimpses none may e’er embrace,  
But hold me captive in a waking dream.  
  Yet though unreal, thy presence I pursue—  
  For in illusions, love may yet be true.

1085

Thou art a phantom clothed in beauty’s guise,  
A wraith that mocks the hunger of mine eyes;  
Thy laughter rings, yet vanishes as mist,  
A fleeting echo lips have never kissed.  
I reach, and shadows slip between my hands,  
Desire dissolves like footprints in the sands.  
The sunbeam finds thee, yet thou turn’st away,  
A star that glimmers only to betray.  
Unbodied sweetness tempts my soul to pine,  
Yet all thy promise withers on the vine.  
My dreams are haunted by thy perfumed breath,  
Each hope, a rose entwined with thorn of death.  
  Yet still, fair specter, I pursue thy shade,  
  Enchanted by the ghostly spell thou’st made.

1086

Within the glass wherein my soul is cast,  
A thousand faces flicker, none my own;  
Reflections shifting, present, future, past,  
Each visage shaped by light and time o’erthrown.  
I search the silver depths for something true,  
A hint of self not borrowed, worn, or feigned,  
Yet every glance reveals another hue,  
A borrowed mask by circumstance ordained.  
What phantom self persists behind the glare,  
When every image fractures, slips, departs?  
Is there a center hidden somewhere there,  
A single pulse beneath a host of parts?  
  Yet still I gaze, and in that searching see  
  A soul refracted into unity.

1087

Within the glass of dreams mine own soul stares,  
Beguiled by phantoms veiled in honeyed lies,  
Each whispered hope dissolved on midnight airs,  
A fleeting wish that in the shadow dies.  
The silent moon doth paint with silver thread  
A tapestry of longing on the floor,  
While restless thoughts, unbidden, softly tread  
Upon the path where sleep unlocks its door.  
Yet every star that kindles in the dark  
Is but a lantern for my wandering mind,  
And every sigh becomes a secret mark  
Of love and loss, in memory entwined.  
  Thus trapped between the night and dawn’s embrace,  
  I search for truth within the dream’s pale face.

1088

Mine own frail soul doth dance on glassy dreams,  
Where shadows mock the light that reason deems.  
A fickle breeze doth stir the silent air,  
And whispers secrets sorrowed hearts forswear.  
The silver moon doth trace her haunted path,  
Her gaze unmoved by joy, untouched by wrath.  
Within the hush of night, my hope is drawn,  
A fragile thread that shivers ere the dawn.  
Yet still I walk, though tempests bar my way,  
In search of truth the stars oft hide away.  
Each step betrays the cracks beneath my feet,  
Yet on I press, though peril courts defeat.  
  For in this maze of doubt and trembling fear,  
  The soul finds strength to rise and persevere.

1089

Mine eyes, deceivèd by thy phantom grace,  
Have led my soul through mists of hollow dreams,  
A willow’s shade upon thy fleeting face,  
Where truth is lesser than its mirror seems.  
Thy voice, a distant bell in midnight air,  
Doth haunt the quiet chambers of my mind,  
Yet every echo finds but sorrow there,  
For hope and memory no concord find.  
These visions, woven from the web of night,  
Dissolve at dawn, as dew upon the rose,  
And leave but shadowed longing in their flight,  
A yearning wound no mortal art may close.  
  Yet, though thy image fades beyond my sight,  
  Love lingers, constant as the stars in night.

1090

What cruelest glass doth mock my longing soul,  
Reflects thy shadow, yet withholds the whole;  
Within its depths thy absent eyes I see,  
A phantom visage haunting silently.  
The silv’red pane distorts thy gentle grace,  
And blurs the mem’ry of thy vanished face,  
Yet still my heart pursues thy fleeting shade,  
As moonlight chases dawn that will not fade.  
O mirror false, thou thief of hope and light,  
Why must thou grant me dreams but not their right?  
Each breath I draw is heavy with thy name,  
Each glance I cast but fans the ceaseless flame.  
  Yet in thy lack, my love is made complete,  
  For shadows prove the sun’s returning heat.

1091

Upon the stage where shadows court my soul,  
False visions dance and mock my heart's control,  
The flick’ring lights that on my spirit toll  
Entangle reason in their subtle scroll.  
Where once the sun did paint with golden hue  
The honest contours of my hopes and fears,  
Now specters murmur secrets never true,  
And laughter masks the silent cry of tears.  
Yet in this play of phantoms, I remain,  
A solitary watcher of the night,  
Who searches through deception’s tangled chain  
For glimmers of an ever-distant light.  
  Though shadows press and falsity may reign,  
  My steadfast heart pursues the truth again.

1092

Within my glass, a stranger mocks my face,  
A phantom draped in veils of borrowed light,  
His eyes reflect a time I can’t replace,  
Yet flicker with the embers of my night.  
He wears my smile but bends it out of tune,  
His laughter rings with echoes not my own.  
The dawn has painted shadows far too soon  
Upon the brow that once was carved from stone.  
He lifts his hand—a gesture slow and strange—  
And shadows break upon the silvered pane.  
Each line and scar, the tokens of my change,  
Are counted by this silent, ghostly chain.  
  Yet though I meet this stranger’s haunting gaze,  
  Within his eyes, my truer self betrays.

1093

This glass, deceitful, shows not what I be,  
But paints a phantom self to mock at me.  
Its silvered grin presents a borrowed face  
That shifts with every mood and fleeting trace.  
My eyes, reflected, seem not quite my own,  
But windows clouded, shadowed, overthrown.  
The lips it shapes are strangers to my thought,  
And all my flaws, exposed, are dearly bought.  
Yet still I gaze, compelled by hope or fear,  
To seek the truth that may not linger here.  
If masks must rule the surface I behold,  
What lies beneath—uncounted and untold?  
  O glass, reveal me not in shallow frame,  
  But let my soul shine through, and know its name.

1094

Upon the stage of glass I tread alone,  
Where shadows mock the truth my heart would own,  
Reflections fracture hope to shards unknown,  
And echo secrets in a brittle tone.  
The lights above like watchful spirits gleam,  
Yet fail to pierce the doubt within my breast;  
Each step resounds the burden of my dream,  
A fragile quest to pass an unseen test.  
The audience, unseen, yet felt in air,  
Awaits the falter or the flight of soul;  
I wear a mask of courage, thin and fair,  
While trembling fears beneath the surface roll.  
  Yet onward still I drift through glass and night,  
  To seek in shards my own emerging light.

1095

Upon a glass of dreams I fix mine eye,  
Yet find my image warped by false delight;  
The silvered veil distorts the truth and sky,  
And twists day’s gold to phantoms of the night.  
I chase the glimmered hope that dances there,  
A spectral promise drifting out of reach,  
Reflected visions shining in the air,  
Each lesson taught that longing cannot teach.  
Yet still I peer, enchanted by the gleam,  
A prisoner of longing’s gentle art,  
For in that glass I see what might have been—  
A shadowed wish that lingers in my heart.  
  So let me gaze, though truth and dream entwine,  
  For in that glass, both loss and hope are mine.

1096

Upon this glass of dreams I gaze in vain,  
Where shadows mock the longing of my heart,  
Reflections wane beneath a silent pane,  
And hopes, like phantoms, flicker then depart.  
The moonlight weaves its silver through my hair,  
Yet yields no answer to my whispered plea;  
Night’s gentle hand erases all my care,  
While stars conceal what fate intends for me.  
I trace the lines that sorrow left behind,  
Soft echoes of a love that would not stay,  
The hourglass spills the moments I would bind,  
As time, relentless, carries them away.  
  Yet in this glass, a promise still appears:  
  That dawn may heal the ache of vanished years.

1097

Upon the stage of hope, mine image plays,  
A phantom clothed in longing’s vain attire,  
Each whispered line a torch that dimly sways,  
Enkindling shadows with unquenched desire.  
The audience of stars attends my plight,  
Their silent witness gilds the trembling air,  
While dreams, like moths, are drawn unto the light  
Of promises that flicker, frail and rare.  
Yet still I speak, though fate would have me mute,  
And stride the boards, despite despair’s command,  
For in each word, a seed takes trembling root,  
And hope’s bright flower blossoms where I stand.  
  So let the curtain fall, or rise anew—  
  My heart remains, steadfast, in longing true.

1098

Illusion weaves her fingers through thy hair,  
And paints thee fairer than the truth would dare.  
Her silver whispers cloud the honest air,  
And mask the visage that thou truly wear.  
With dreams she drapes thy image, soft and light,  
And leads fond eyes astray from what is real.  
A glamour spins around thee in the night,  
Concealing flaws that daylight might reveal.  
Yet shadows linger, silent in their scheme,  
Awaiting dawn to strip the veil away.  
For beauty forged within the forge of dream  
Must meet the sun and falter in its ray.  
  Yet love, though truth may banish phantoms spun,  
  Admires the heart, when all illusions run.

1099

O Illusion, thou dost mask my lover’s face,  
With honeyed lies that vex my heart’s repose,  
Thy gossamer deceit doth time efface,  
And paints false roses where no true one grows.  
Thou art a veil the longing soul adorns,  
A shifting shadow cast on hopeful eyes,  
In midnight’s hush, thy subtle magic warns  
That dreams may waft as swiftly as they rise.  
Yet, in thy grasp, my senses sweetly dwell,  
For bitter truth, though bright, doth oft offend;  
I’d rather feast upon thy gentle spell  
Than taste the dawn that brings illusions’ end.  
  So, let me love the mask, though frail and thin,  
  For sometimes lies are kinder than what’s been.

1100

Thou art the wily Dream that mocks my sight,  
With painted smiles that mask a heart untrue,  
Thy phantom fingers weave the threads of night  
Yet vanish swift ere morning brings its hue.  
In silken whispers dost thou haunt my sleep,  
A shadow perched upon my weary breast,  
With promises thou never mean’st to keep  
And honeyed lies that bid my soul no rest.  
Thy beauty glimmers like a distant star  
That dances just beyond my outstretched hand;  
I chase, and find how vain such longings are,  
For thou art mist that will not heed command.  
  Yet still I court thee, though thy truth I guess—  
  For hope is sweeter than the heart’s distress.

1101

Illusion weaves her silken, biting thread,  
Around thy heart, where hope and longing bled.  
She dances soft upon the wistful mind,  
And paints false dawns that promise truth behind.  
Her whispers, fragile as a captive breeze,  
Unravel certainties with gentle art;  
She sprinkles moonlit dust upon the trees,  
And plants deceptive seeds within the heart.  
Desire, a moth, pursues her phantom gleam,  
Its wings singed bright by dreams that cannot stay;  
Yet in the labyrinth of half-lit dream,  
We chase her shadow further every day.  
  Though reason’s lantern seeks to break her spell,  
  We drink her honeyed lies and love them well.

1102

Thou art but Mirage, fair as dawn's deceit,  
Who weaves soft lies in every glance I meet.  
Thy laughter rings as chimes through summer air,  
Yet vanishes when I would hold it near.  
A shadow cast by hopes that dare compete  
With daylight’s truth, yet perish in their fear.  
Thy promise gleams where longing and dreams greet,  
But melts away if I should venture near.  
I chase thy phantom footfalls down the lane,  
Where petals fall and silent wishes lie,  
And find but echoes, empty of thy name,  
A whisper lost beneath the restless sky.  
  Yet still I love thee, vision, false and fleet,  
  For dreams are sweeter oft than truths we meet.

1103

Thou art a phantom draped in sugared light,  
Whose honeyed words do mock my longing soul,  
Thy laughter dances on the edge of night,  
Yet leaves my heart unsated, never whole.  
A wraith of promise, sweet in fleeting grace,  
Thou vanish’st ere the dawn can touch thy cheek;  
I chase the shadowed memory of thy face,  
While silence grows where once I heard thee speak.  
Thine eyes, twin lanterns in the mist, betray  
A world of secrets veiled in golden haze;  
Yet every step I take is led astray,  
Ensnared within the labyrinth of thy gaze.  
  O, if thy phantom form could flesh become,  
  My soul, unmocked, would find its truest home.

1104

Illusion weaves her silken threads within  
My soul, a jester locked behind grim skin.  
She dances shadows where my hopes have been,  
And paints with moonlight all the fears I spin.  
Her laughter echoes in the halls of thought,  
A masquerade of truth and sweet deceit;  
Each promise given, swiftly overwrought  
By whispers winding swift upon her feet.  
I chase her glimpses through the mirrored haze,  
Yet grasp but phantoms, trembling and unsure;  
The dawn reveals the folly of her maze,  
Yet still her touch I helplessly endure.  
  For in the theater of dreams she reigns—  
  A sovereign crafting joy from silent pains.

1105

Thou painted shadow, falsehood’s gentle guise,  
Dost lead my soul through corridors of lies,  
A whisper soft that sweetly doth disguise  
The spectral truth that ever silent flies.  
Thy honeyed words, like summer’s balmy breeze,  
Do lull the wary heart to careless rest,  
Yet underneath thy charm, a poison sees  
The innocence within my wounded breast.  
Yet still I chase thy ever-shifting shade,  
Compelled by hope, though reason bids me cease;  
For in thy darkness, fleeting dreams are made,  
And with their fading, all my doubts increase.  
  O shadowed mask, thou art both curse and cure—  
  For love and longing make thy falsehood pure.

1106

Thou painted shade, thou jester to my soul,  
That weav’st thy webs where honest thoughts should dwell,  
Thy laughter rings where silent tempests roll,  
A maskèd truth no searching glance can quell.  
Thine artful tongue with honeyed venom stings,  
Disguising wounds beneath a courtly jest;  
Thy dance upon the edge of midnight brings  
A restless longing, never granting rest.  
Thou mock’st the light with shadows deftly drawn,  
And steal’st the bloom of hope with idle cheer.  
Thou art the specter haunting every dawn,  
A whispering doubt that ever lingers near.  
  Yet in thy folly, wisdom oft is found:  
  A mirror cracked, where truer selves resound.

1107

Illusion weaves her silver threads around  
My hapless soul in bitter longing bound;  
She paints with moonlit brush on shadowed ground  
A hope where only empty dreams are found.  
Her whispered promises, like perfumed air,  
Entangle sense and reason in their flight,  
And conjure phantoms beautiful and fair  
That vanish with the dawning of the light.  
Yet, still I chase the visions that she spins,  
Entranced by every fleeting, spectral gleam;  
Though every chase another loss begins,  
I cannot wake me from this waking dream.  
  So let me dwell within her ghostly art,  
  For sweet illusion soothes my aching heart.

1108

Illusion weaves her fingers through my soul,  
And mocks my heart with dreams that cannot be,  
She paints a dawn where night retains control,  
And blinds my sight with veils I scarce can see.  
Her whispers promise fortune, sweet and bright,  
Yet lead me down a labyrinth of mist;  
She borrows hope and steals away the light,  
And leaves me longing for what I have missed.  
Yet still I chase her shadow through the day,  
Entranced by visions spun from golden thread,  
Believing truth must surely find its way  
Within the lies her honeyed tongue has spread.  
  O let me wake, and cast her chains aside,  
  For only then may peace in me abide.

1109

Illusion weaves her veils about my soul,  
With sugared lies that leave my spirit cold,  
She paints the dawn with promises she stole,  
And turns bright hopes to tarnished, faded gold.  
Her whispers thread like silk through waking thought,  
Enticing reason down uncertain ways,  
Till what is real and what is fabled, fraught,  
Converge in dusk and blur the edge of days.  
I chase the shadows dancing on the wall,  
Mistaking shapes for things that cannot be,  
And, lost within her labyrinthine thrall,  
Grow blind to doors that might have set me free.  
  Yet through her mists, a steadfast truth I find—  
  The heart knows light no phantom can outbind.

1110

Illusion, thou dost haunt mine inward eye,  
A false-tongued shade that mocks my waking mind,  
With veiled allure, thou bid’st sweet hopes to fly  
And leave the form of truth in mist confined.  
Like moonlight’s dance upon a restless sea,  
Thy phantom hand doth stir my heart’s unrest,  
While reason’s lamp burns low and ceaselessly  
I chase the shimmer thou hast deftly dressed.  
Yet in thy grasp, the world is not the same;  
The rose is fairer, night more velvet grows,  
But fleeting is thy softly-spoken name,  
And dawn reveals the falseness thou bestows.  
  Still, gentle wraith, I bless thy fleeting art—  
  For dreams, though false, bring solace to my heart.

1111

Thou fickle Illusion, thou dost haunt my mind,  
Thy silver tongue with venom is entwined.  
Thou paint’st the world with colors false and bright,  
Yet in thy glow, all truths are left behind.  
Upon the dawn, thy shadow slips from sight,  
But in the dusk, thou dost my thoughts confound.  
Sweet promises thou whisper’st in the night,  
Yet leave me waking, empty and unbound.  
With every dream, thy gentle touch is near,  
And lures my heart to wander, lost and blind.  
Thy laughter dances softly on mine ear,  
A melody that reason cannot bind.  
  Yet though I chase thee, ever out of view,  
  Thy absence proves thy presence never true.

1112

Illusion weaves her silk about my mind,  
Her honeyed tongue with bitter draughts entwined,  
She paints a dawn where midnight hours unwind,  
And cloaks the truth so it is never mined.  
Her whispers, soft, like petals in the breeze,  
Seduce my senses, blinding every eye;  
A thousand dreams she conjures with such ease,  
Yet each dissolves beneath the morning sky.  
She leads me through a garden choked with mist,  
Where shadows dance in masquerading light.  
Her phantom promises, too sweet to resist,  
Turn gold to dust before the coming night.  
  Yet still I chase her, knowing all the while  
  Deceit wears beauty’s ever-changing smile.

1113

Illusion, thou dost haunt my sleepless mind,  
A mistress false, in shadows thou art dressed,  
Thy silken words around my thoughts entwined,  
With phantoms sweet my weary soul impressed.  
Thou paint’st with golden light a world untrue,  
And bid’st me wander halls that cannot be,  
Where hopes arise as morning’s tender dew,  
And vanish swift as wind upon the sea.  
Yet in thy arms, a gentle peace I find,  
Though reason’s torch would chase thee from my sight,  
I yield to dreams that in thy bosom bind,  
And taste the honeyed edge of false delight.  
  O, let me linger in thy soft embrace,  
  For truth and waking bear a harsher face.

1114

Illusion weaves her web about my heart,  
With bitter threads she sews my hope anew,  
She spins her silken lies with cunning art,  
And paints the sky in ever-changing hue.  
Her phantom hands do cradle every thought,  
And whisper dreams that flicker, then depart,  
She binds my will, in shadowed longing caught,  
Yet leaves behind the ache her ghosts impart.  
I chase her mirage through the lonely night,  
While reason’s lamp is shuttered by her veil,  
Each promise shimmers just beyond my sight,  
And all my certainties begin to pale.  
  Yet though her snares have torn my soul in two,  
  Still, in her web, I find my solace too.

1115

Fair Illusion, dost thou mock my longing gaze,  
And paint false hope upon my weary brow?  
Thy silken whispers lead me through a maze  
Where dreams take flight, but truth will not allow.  
Thou dressest shadows in a golden hue,  
Ensnaring hearts that seek the morning sun;  
Yet when I reach, thy form dissolves from view,  
Leaving me grasping what cannot be won.  
Thy honeyed promises, so softly spun,  
Entangle reason in a web of night;  
I chase thee still, though knowing I am undone,  
For absence sharpens hunger past delight.  
  Yet in thy dance, sweet phantoms rise and fall—  
  A fleeting song, and I am captive thrall.

1116

Thou art a shadow draped in golden light,  
A fevered dream that mocks my waking soul,  
Between the dusk and dawn’s uncertain flight,  
Thy visage haunts me, never fully whole.  
Thine eyes, twin lanterns set in midnight’s veil,  
Do spark the embers of my restless mind;  
Thy whispered words, like winds that softly wail,  
Entwine my thoughts, in memory confined.  
Yet, as the sun doth chase the moon away,  
So reason seeks to banish thee anew;  
But heart is captive, longing still to stay  
Within thy presence, bathed in mist and dew.  
  Though thou art fleeting as the morning dew,  
  My soul remains, forever bound to you.

1117

Thy visage fair, a mask o’er hollow shade,  
Where truth and falsehood war within my breast,  
A gentle smile, in borrowed light arrayed,  
Conceals the tempest none may have confessed.  
Thy eyes, twin lanterns, flicker soft and bright,  
Yet hide the secrets trembling in their fire;  
They draw my spirit forth into the night,  
And set my soul alight with frail desire.  
In whispered words, thy tongue both soothes and stings,  
A melody of comfort and deceit;  
The hope thou bring’st is borne on fragile wings,  
Too soon to falter, destined to retreat.  
  Yet, though I know thy love may be untrue,  
  My heart, a willing captive, cleaves to you.

1118

Thou art the shade that mocks my longing sight,  
Yet shines with hues that ever prove untrue,  
A fleeting dream that dances with the night,  
And slips through grasping fingers like the dew.  
Thy laughter rings as music on the breeze,  
Yet leaves my soul in silence once it fades;  
Thy promises are leaves on autumn trees,  
Whose colors fade as hope within me wades.  
Thy presence, like a phantom in the dawn,  
Doth haunt the chambers of my waking mind,  
And all the light I seek is quickly gone,  
As shadows lengthen, leaving naught behind.  
  Yet still my heart, ensnared by beauty’s art,  
  Remains thy captive, prisoner to thy heart.

1119

Thine image fair, a phantom in mine eye,  
Yet all thy whispered vows prove but a lie,  
Thy honeyed words, like dew at dawn, do fade,  
And leave me cold beneath the willow’s shade.  
Thou once didst paint my soul with hues of gold,  
Yet now thy touch is distant, wan, and cold.  
As fleeting as the moon upon the tide,  
Thy promises like autumn leaves have died.  
Oft have I searched the silence for thy voice,  
And found within the hush no cause rejoice.  
False hope, like morning mist, dissolves in light,  
Revealing only absence in my sight.  
  Yet still my heart, though reason bids it part,  
  Clings to thy memory, and mourns its art.

1120

Thy eyes, sweet veils, do mask a heart of stone,  
Whilst honeyed words conceal a will unkind,  
A gentle smile, in shadowed meaning grown,  
Doth cloak the tempest boiling in thy mind.  
Thy laughter, silver bells at eventide,  
Distracts the world from schemes that thou dost weave;  
With every touch, thy secret truths you hide,  
And bid the trusting soul in dreams believe.  
Yet through thy art, some cracks begin to show,  
A fleeting glance betrays what lies beneath;  
The chill of winter in thy bosom's flow,  
The serpent's tongue concealed by rosy wreath.  
  Still, beauty veils thy coldness, fair but dire—  
  A rose whose thorns consume with silent fire.

1121

Thy visage fair, a mask in beauty’s stead,  
Yet truth behind thy eyes lies cold and dead.  
The rose upon thy cheek, though sweetly red,  
Conceals the thorns where gentler hopes have bled.  
Thy honeyed words, like music softly played,
